Design for scripted-demo changes:

```ts
/** 每个 plan 的「原句」与三档改写后的句子：第二场简历里出现的可能是其中任何一句 */
function quotesOf(plan: DemoClaimPlan): string[] {
  const out = [plan.quote];
  const suggestion = plan.suggestion;
  if (suggestion !== null) {
    out.push(suggestion.after);
    for (const style of REWRITE_STYLES) {
      const variant = suggestion.afterByStyle[style];
      if (variant !== undefined && out.indexOf(variant) < 0) out.push(variant);
    }
  }
  return out;
}
```

```ts
function extractText(prompt: string, state: ScriptState): string {
  const second = isRevisedPrompt(prompt);
  state.mode = second ? 'second' : 'first';
  if (!second) { ...existing... }
  const claims = [];
  for (const plan of DEMO_PLANS) {

    if (quote === null) continue;    // 改完之后这条不再出现
    claims.push({ text: revisedTextOf(plan, quote), quote, category: plan.category, weight: plan.weight });
  }
  return JSON.stringify({ claims });
}
```

So per-claim resolution handles partial acceptance well:

```ts
function quoteFor(plan, prompt): string | null {
  if (prompt.indexOf(plan.quote) >= 0) return plan.quote;         // 原句还在 → 用它
  for (const cand of afterCandidates(plan)) if (prompt.indexOf(cand) >= 0) return cand;
  return null;  // 两种都不在（用户手工改过）→ 这条不抽
}
```

```ts
const LINE_PREFIX = /^\s*(?:-\s+|\[[^\]]*\]\s*)/;
function lineContaining(prompt: string, core: string): string | null {
  const at = prompt.indexOf(core);
  if (at < 0) return null;
  const start = prompt.lastIndexOf(NL, at) + 1;
  let end = prompt.indexOf(NL, at + core.length);
  if (end < 0) end = prompt.length;
  const raw = prompt.slice(start, end).trim();
  return raw.replace(/^\[[^\]]*\]\s*/, '').replace(/^-\s*/, '');
}
```

Now: how do I make demo-seed do this? In `runOne`, after the record run, do `acceptRevisions(recordDb, sessionId1, allSuggestionClaimIds)` then create session2 with resumeText = revised text, parentSessionId = session1, then run again with a *fresh* scripted client (variant revised) — but the entries come from one client instance per combo... Currently `runOne` takes `scripted` (a single client). I need two clients: `scripted1` (original variant) and `scripted2` (revised variant). Both write to the same dir and both return entries. So `seedDemoCassettes` creates both and merges entries.

Also the replay side needs 2 sessions in a fresh :memory: db, and signature comparison for both.

Design for runOne:

```
async function runOne(dir, scripted1, scripted2, difficulty, style) {
  // record db
  const recordDb = openDatabase({path: ':memory:'});
  const sid1 = newSessionId(recordDb, 'record', difficulty, style);
  const firstRun = await runAndPersist({db: recordDb, sessionId: sid1, client: scripted1.client, model: 'scripted-demo'});
  const accepted = firstRun.suggestions.map(s => s.claimId);  // all
  const acceptedResult = acceptRevisions(recordDb, sid1, accepted);
  if (!acceptedResult.ok) throw...
  const revisedText = acceptedResult.view.text;
  const sid2 = newSessionId(recordDb, 'record', difficulty, style, {resumeText: revisedText, parentSessionId: sid1});
  const secondRun = await runAndPersist(...client: scripted2.client...);
  // replay db: same sequence with createReplayClient
  ...
}
```

`newSessionId` needs to accept optional resumeText + parentSessionId. Also `resumeHash` from the actual text.

Also signature: compare signatureOf(firstRun)+signatureOf(secondRun) between record and replay. And check second run has improved claims (e.g. secondRun.verdicts has more solid than first) — a nice self-check: "第二场的站得住必须比第一场多" (proves the demo story).

Report shape: `DemoRun` needs to expose second-run summary and the compare info. bin/seed-demo.ts prints them. Also DemoSeedReport.record/replay are used by tests.

But the quotes must match the revised text which the seed computes at runtime. So: seed computes revised text (via applyRevisions on DEMO_RESUME with all 4 suggestions for that style), then **derives the new quotes** by applying the same replacements to the plan quotes:

For each plan with a suggestion, the revised line = original quote with `before` replaced by `after`. Since suggestion.before is a substring of plan.quote (C2: 'QPS 从 800 提升到 5000' ⊂ '主导引入 Redis 缓存，QPS 从 800 提升到 5000。' ✓; C3: before is the entire quote ✓; C4: entire quote ✓; C5: entire quote ✓). So the scripted demo can compute `revisedQuote = plan.quote.replace(plan.suggestion.before, plan.suggestion.after)`.

That's deterministic and self-consistent — no hand-authored second set! And the seed does the same via applyRevisions, which produces exactly the same text (same before/after/positions).

### 1. `packages/server/src/llm/scripted-demo.ts`

- `createDemoScriptedClient(dir, options?: { phase?: DemoPhase; style?: RewriteStyle })`.
- In rerun phase:
  - `extractText(style)`: claims from `rerunPlansFor(style)` + DEMO_UNANCHORED_DRAFT.
  - `probeText`: uses rerun plans' questions; one question per claim (cap).
  - `answerText`: rerun plan's answerText with citedQuotes = [rerunQuote].
  - `verdictText`: kind solid, confidence 0.8x, rationale = rerun.rationale, evidenceQuotes = [rerunQuote].
  - `suggestText`: never called (all solid) — but if called, throw informative error.

  But careful: does applyRevisions replace exactly that span? `revisionOf` uses `suggestion.anchor` (resolved from before in the *session resume*) + after. So revised quote = quote with that span replaced. If before appears once, identical. ✓.
  However: the second run's claim quote must be found in the revised resume — yes.
  But one more subtlety: the *revised* line for C2 in conservative style is longer; the claim quote in extract is the full line minus trailing '。'? plan.quote includes '。' and so does the revised. Fine.

- `reasoningFor`: rerun verdict thought — use solid thought.

```ts
/** 演示里真正会被采纳的四条改写建议（C1 站得住，本来就没有建议） */
function adoptedSuggestions(style: RewriteStyle): { plan: DemoClaimPlan; suggestion: DemoSuggestionPlan; after: string; at: number }[] {
  const out = [];
  for (const plan of DEMO_PLANS) {
    if (plan.suggestion === null) continue;
    const after = plan.suggestion.afterByStyle[style] ?? plan.suggestion.after;
    const at = DEMO_RESUME.indexOf(plan.suggestion.before);
    if (at < 0) continue;
    out.push({ plan, suggestion: plan.suggestion, after, at });
  }
  return out;
}

export function revisedDemoResume(style: RewriteStyle): string {
  const revisions: Revision[] = adoptedSuggestions(style).map((item) => ({
    claimId: item.plan.text, // claimId 只需要可辨
    start: item.at,
    end: item.at + item.suggestion.before.length,
    before: item.suggestion.before,
    after: item.after,
  }));
  return applyRevisions(DEMO_RESUME, revisions).text;
}
```

注意 Revision 的 claimId 类型是 string，ok。

实现：

```ts
/** 演示里会被采纳的那几条改写建议（C1 站得住，本来就没有建议） */
function adoptedFor(style: RewriteStyle): { claimId: string; before: string; after: string; start: number }[] {
  const out: { claimId: string; before: string; after: string; start: number }[] = [];
  DEMO_PLANS.forEach((plan, index) => {
    if (plan.suggestion === null) return;
    const before = plan.suggestion.before;
    const start = DEMO_RESUME.indexOf(before);
    if (start < 0) return;
    out.push({
      claimId: 'C' + String(index + 1),
      before,
      after: plan.suggestion.afterByStyle[style] ?? plan.suggestion.after,
      start,
    });
  });
  return out;
}

/** 改完之后的演示简历：与界面上「全部采纳」得到的是同一份文本 */
export function revisedDemoResume(style: RewriteStyle): string {
  const revisions: Revision[] = adoptedFor(style).map((item) => ({
    claimId: item.claimId,
    start: item.start,
    end: item.start + item.before.length,
    before: item.before,
    after: item.after,
  }));
  return applyRevisions(DEMO_RESUME, revisions).text;
}
```

然后 rerun claim drafts:

```ts
export function demoRerunDrafts(style: RewriteStyle): DemoClaimDraft[] {
  const revised = revisedDemoResume(style);
  const drafts: DemoClaimDraft[] = [];
  for (const plan of DEMO_RERUN_PLANS) { ... }
}
```

每个 rerun plan 有 quoteAfter / 或 before（对应第一场的 before）。简单点：rerun plan 带 `quoteOf: (style) => string`？不好序列化。

改为：rerun plan 里带 `source: 'keep' | 'after'`：
- C1 → 用 DEMO_PLANS[0].quote
- 其余 → adoptedFor(style) 里对应 claimId 的 after

所以：

```ts
function rerunQuoteOf(claimId: string, style: RewriteStyle): string {
  const adopted = adoptedFor(style);
  const hit = adopted.find((item) => item.claimId === claimId);
  if (hit !== undefined) return hit.after;
  const plan = planForClaimId(claimId);
  return plan === null ? '' : plan.quote;
}
```

然后 rerun plan 用 claimId 索引。

extract 第二场：

```ts
function rerunExtractText(style: RewriteStyle): string {
  const claims: DemoClaimDraft[] = [];
  for (const plan of DEMO_RERUN_PLANS) {
    const quote = rerunQuoteOf(plan.claimId, style);
    if (quote === '') continue;
    claims.push({ text: plan.text, quote, category: ..., weight: ... });
  }
  return JSON.stringify({ claims });
}
```

```ts
function quoteForMarker(prompt: string, marker: string): string {
  const at = prompt.indexOf(marker);
  if (at < 0) return '';
  const nl = prompt.indexOf(NL, at);
  return (nl < 0 ? prompt.slice(at) : prompt.slice(at, nl)).trim();
}
```

}

Actually simpler: in session 2, counterText uses `claimIdForQuote` but for RESUME-sourced plans where quote is missing, fall back to the rerun marker quote. Let me map: process plan → C3 → marker 'RBAC 权限模块'. growth → C1 → '主导重构订单系统'.

Implementation for one db:

```ts
async function runPair(
  db: AnyDb,
  dir: string | null,
  clients: { first: LlmClient; second: LlmClient },
  model: string,
  difficulty: Difficulty,
  style: RewriteStyle,
): Promise<{ first: RunSessionResult; second: RunSessionResult; rerunResume: string }> {
  const firstId = newSessionId(db, mode, difficulty, style);
  const first = await runAndPersist({db, sessionId: firstId, client: clients.first, model});
  const accepted = acceptAllRevisions(db, firstId);
  const rerunResume = accepted === null ? DEMO_RESUME : accepted;
  const secondId = newSessionId(db, mode, difficulty, style, { resumeText: rerunResume, parentSessionId: firstId });
  const second = await runAndPersist({db, sessionId: secondId, client: clients.second, model});
  return { first, second };
}
```

Hmm but `mode` differs (record/replay) for the two dbs. Keep newSessionId(db, mode, ...).

acceptAll: 
```ts
function acceptAllRevisions(db: AnyDb, sessionId: string): string | null {
  const ids = listClaims(db, sessionId).filter(c => c.suggestion !== null).map(c => c.id);
  if (ids.length === 0) return null;
  const result = acceptRevisions(db, sessionId, ids);
  if (!result.ok) return null;
  return result.view.text;
}
```
